# Campaign ledger: every sector as a campaign that asked the parent question

Packet `grut_closed_whole_theory_20261019`. Each sector of the GRUT record was re-read as one campaign
asking: *what does a closed whole, whose subsystem baths are internal complements, force beyond known
physics, in this place?* Each entry below was written by one reader from the primary sources. Entries
s01–s10 were then re-read adversarially by a second reader against the same sources, and the entry shown
is the corrected one (s10's corrected entry is embedded verbatim from `ledger/`). Entries s11–s12 are
single-reader entries; their full re-reads did not complete (session limits). The report claims drawn
from s10–s12 were checked against the sources in the main session (record:
`results/verification/s10_s12_row_checks.md`), and the round-2 attack on the packet caught further
errors in them, now corrected. Several sectors did not themselves pose the closed-whole question
(s01, s03, s04, s11, s12: open or classical classes, declared baths, or program ledgers); their
verdicts are re-readings in the program's own terms, not closure demonstrations. Full entries are under
`ledger/`. Classifications: recovered known physics · conditional constraint · supplied model choice ·
rejected mechanism · conjecture · unexecuted task · mixed.

Closure 'fixes neither the observer's restriction nor its thermal spectrum' (DERIVATION.md:50-132; results.json:193-203, :367-377; REPORT.md:85-91); (iii) the exact closed-de-Sitter identity Omega_K=-1/(e^{2N}-1) (-4.5401991009687765e-05 at N=5, -2.061153626686912e-09 at N=10) and the flat torus (Ricci scalar 0) show that closure and compactness force neither the curvature sign nor its magnitude (results.json:383-430); (iv) the exactly soluble closed case, with Gibbons-Hawking imported, shows the flat temperature formula is off by sqrt(1+|Omega_K|), with discrepancy/|Omega_K| -> 0.5 (0.49999432487995393 at N=5) (results.json:389-423); (v) the only positive-curvature derivation found is conditional on the original global sequestering equation with finite integrals, a vanishing endpoint term and treated singular endpoints. That is a supplied global modification, not closure (DERIVATION.md:199-215). The sources add that this is not a proof that no future explicit postulate could have content: 'This does not declare the program permanently impossible. **Answer.** No. On the repository's record the CTP doubling is the standard Feynman–Vernon / Keldysh form that any local causal open system yields (provenance/claims.json:638-655); it does not select the dynamics (GR borrowed, NO_GO_LEDGER.md:59-64; temporal generator supplied, GRUT_WORKING_THEORY_SYNTHESIS_01.md:43), does not fix its own kernel family (S_IF.md:144-151), yields no structure from its own form beyond a presupposed adiabatic mode (calc/RESULTS_L0_redundancy.md:23-33), and does not derive the Born measure (NO_GO_LEDGER.md:66-71; GRUT_PROGRAM_CLOSURE_01.md:38-43; GRUT_III/01_PRINCIPLES.md:66-74). No record uses CTP normalization as a derivation route; the KMS lock and the arrow-existence half are graded standard/imported (GRUT_III/04_LEDGER_MIGRATION.md:14; docs/WHERE_IT_STOPS.md:197). The response vocabulary is 'not physical content' (GRUT_WORKING_THEORY_01.md:84-87) and its one candidate principle is NULL-AS-NEW-PRINCIPLE (:162). The predicted column is empty (GRUT_MODEL_FRAMEWORK.md:86; GRUT_PROGRAM_STATE_SYNTHESIS_01.md:164-165). Later records grade the organization successively as a stance with declared inputs, borrowed-and-verified legs, a Bin-1 bet, a negative-results report, a constitutive framework, a retired ToE claim, a dependency map over nine supplied layers, and in GRUT III an identification inside P0 with S_IF the quadratic approximation of a computed Feynman–Vernon functional. S_IF is downstream of the global law and coupling (P0, P3), a partition/sector choice, a state, access, a supplied quantum lift and ħ; the record's own noise-origin campaign finds the CTP records 'declare no parent' (S2_NOISE_ORIGIN_DEPOSIT_02.md:34). Whether V7 itself asserted anything beyond this cannot be checked: the document is absent from every ref of the repository.

**Closure verdict.** Nothing additional was forced in the stated class. The CTP / S_IF organization is recovered known physics applied to supplied choices, and the repository's own computations show it: (1) form universality, S_IF = K_R + (i/2)N generic, ledger 0 (provenance/claims.json:638-655); (2) the in-in machinery does no work at the gravity rung, EH being one of many Ward-consistent actions (NO_GO_LEDGER.md:59-64; provenance/claims.json:117-133); (3) the doubled-contour action with S1–S7 declared 'fixes nothing' in its modulus space (S_IF.md:144-151), and linearized EH itself is ½k²[P⁽²⁾ − 2P⁽⁰ˢ⁾], ratio −2 (docs/WHERE_IT_STOPS.md:26); (4) the dilatation redundancy of S_IF rests on a presupposed adiabatic mode and L₀ is not the unique breaker (calc/RESULTS_L0_redundancy.md:23-33); (5) the exactly solvable arrow model: Γ(t) even, full recurrence at t_rec ∝ N, decay set by the assumed state (GRUT_ToE.md:160; docs/WHERE_IT_STOPS.md:165-172); (6) decoherence derived exactly but no outcome or weights, EXPERIMENT_P_FRONTIER = CLOSED_AT_TESTED_MODEL_CLASS (GRUT_PROGRAM_CLOSURE_01.md:38-43); (7) hierarchy positivity reduces to state positivity, P-4 21/22, NULL-AS-NEW-PRINCIPLE (GRUT_WORKING_THEORY_01.md:155-165); (8) every differentiator fails by computed magnitude: 7–47, ~22–62 (printed 21–62 elsewhere), 62.7 orders below threshold, 689 Hz withdrawn, 32σ → 2.0σ (GRUT_ToE.md:166; NO_GO_LEDGER.md:54; GRUT_FINAL_SYNTHESIS_01.md:167-170; GRUT_PROGRAM_CLOSURE_01.md:33-37). Boundary: none of these took 'bath = rest of the universe' as premise; they concern a declared split whose bath is 'integrated out and NOT specified' (S_IF.md:21-24) and for which the record declares 'no parent' (S2_HB_OWNER_RULING_02.md:56). This sector has no executed closed-whole computation; the handoff's closed-whole paragraphs are 'a construction brief, not a newly executed general theorem' (handoff:107-109,202-203). 'Nothing additional was forced' is a result, 'not proof that no possible future extra postulate could have content' (handoff:128-129).

**Classification.** `mixed` — Recovered known physics: CTP/Feynman–Vernon form (provenance/claims.json:638-655); KMS/FDT lock (GRUT_III/04_LEDGER_MIGRATION.md:14,38); arrow-existence half (docs/WHERE_IT_STOPS.md:153-155,197); GR recovery-with-imports (provenance/claims.json:117-133); Schrödinger core plus decoherence rate (NO_GO_LEDGER.md:66-71); L₀→0 redundancy leg R1 (calc/RESULTS_L0_redundancy.md:13). ### s02_influence_hierarchy — Influence hierarchy / dependence campaigns (kernel, noise, positivity, higher influence data)  (`s02_influence_hierarchy`)

**Question asked.** When a subsystem's bath is the rest of the universe and an observer couples to it only through a declared access structure, what does closure force about the subsystem's influence data (retarded kernel K, noise kernel N, higher connected influence cumulants, and their admissibility) beyond the quantum theory of the closed global system restricted through that coupling? Sub-questions: (a) are kernel and noise downstream of subsystem, state and access choices; (b) does matching two-point data fix higher influence data; (c) are the positivity constraints anything beyond quantum state positivity; (d) what did each verdict establish and in what class. Record framing: 'microscopic dynamics -> influence hierarchy -> sector structure -> access structure? -> effective physics' (P5_ACCESS_CHARTER_01.md:13-14), closing after P-6 as 'access seed = non-derived input' (P6_SEED_SELECTION_VERDICT_01.md:105-110).

**Assumptions / model class.**
- All results are in finite declared classes: D-1 Gaussian class, factorized initial state, |S|=1, finite 9-/11-dim baths (DISCRIMINATOR_VERDICT_01.md:8-9,70-72); P-2 scalar S channel, Gaussian, finite-mode, 'borrowed-standard Gaussian realizability mathematics', 165 sectors = 55 partitions x 3 P-1 worlds (P2_INFLUENCE_CONE_VERDICT_01.md:8-9,41-42; P2_INFLUENCE_CONE_RESULT.json:133); P-3/P-4/P-5 exact finite spin baths with dephasing probe in the 'declared finite bounded-access class' (P4_HIERARCHY_CHARTER_01.md:11-13; P5_ACCESS_CHARTER_01.md:6-7,25-71); P-6 exact finite spin systems, noncommuting seed algebras, entropy/complexity/least-action/simplicity selectors prohibited (P6_SEED_SELECTION_CHARTER_01.md:17-26,38-110).
- C1-a/C1-a2: finite first-order passive local networks, stepped modulation, declared window (C1_STATIONARITY_SEAM_VERDICT_01.md:88-93; C1A2_PACKAGING_VERDICT_01.md:90-92). Kernel transport / nonstationarity: free TT graviton commutator kernel per comoving k on declared LCDM Om=0.315, OL=0.685, fixed-comoving-k object, no smearing (KERNEL_TRANSPORT_RESULT.json:8-11,234; KERNEL_NONSTATIONARITY_RESULT.json:282). EQ-1: 1+1 only, declared five-direction family (EQ1_EQUIVALENCE_VERDICT_01.md:118-123). S4-1: finite chains L=8, range<=3 (S41_SEL4_VERDICT_01.md:89-97). FS-1: exact phonon ring N=20, quadratic translation-invariant charges to range 4, 'Everything below is conditional on C_cons' (FS1_FREE_VERDICT_01.md:10-11,31-34,85-87). CA-1: 1D ring substrates, IR static and short-time data (CA1_CARRIER_VERDICT_01.md:80-81). S2 chain: declared nonlinear C-B class with supplied L0-1c drift (S2_OWNER_RULING_03.md:47-49).
- Supplied inputs presupposed throughout: physical hbar ('located, not generated', P2_S1_OWNER_RULING_01.md:37-43); the access seed as a declaration / irreducible input (P5_ACCESS_VERDICT_01.md:35-38; G1_GEOMETRY_CHARTER_01.md:4-5); the quantum class/lift (S5_OWNER_RULING_01.md:27-35); on FRW a state choice, a KMS/temperature structure and a stationary reduction (KERNEL_TRANSPORT_VERDICT_01.md:41-51; GRUT_FORMALIZATION_02.md:109); on the gravity side Sel-4, C_cons as supplied probe field content, GeoInv (EQ1_EQUIVALENCE_VERDICT_01.md:13-22; CC1_CCONS_VERDICT_01.md:15-17; U1_UNIVERSALITY_CHARTER_01.md:3-9).
- The influence functional is computed, not postulated: 'the reduced probe dynamics is a functional of the contour-ordered influence functional BY CONSTRUCTION (Feynman-Vernon at operator level)' (P4_HIERARCHY_CHARTER_01.md:74-77); GRUT III P1 'supplies no influence functional and no dynamics' and 'No kernel is supplied anywhere; nothing is earned by that fact alone, because the kernels that replace it are standard physics' (GRUT_III/01_PRINCIPLES.md:29-31,86-88).

**Executed evidence.**
- D-1 (13/13): non-isomorphic worlds (9-dim vs 11-dim) with identical (K,N) are S-indistinguishable; kernel-equal state-different worlds are distinguishable only through N; 'The equivalence-class label is the pair (K, N) — in non-Gaussian worlds, the full influence functional — never K alone.' — `DISCRIMINATOR_VERDICT_01.md:6,20-47,60-67; DISCRIMINATOR_D1_RESULT.json:12,22,37,57,62,84-104; D1_P2_OWNER_RULING_01.md:9-10,18-19` — Delta_K = 6.3e-15, Delta_N < 1e-8, Delta_1 = 4.5e-15, Delta_2 = 6.3e-15, W-D: Delta_K = 0.0 (same K, thermal state; 'state-independence of the kernel'), noise kernel differs by 1.09, Delta_1(A,D) = 0.91

**Answer.** (a) Yes for the influence functional, with two precisions. The influence data are the Feynman-Vernon output of a supplied (global law, state, coupling) (P4_HIERARCHY_CHARTER_01.md:74-77; GRUT_III/01_PRINCIPLES.md:29-31,41-43). The subsystem criterion moves a sector to a different point of the same cone (P-2 L-D, 165 sectors); the access seed is a declaration that no tested dynamical selector fixes beyond block decomposition (P-5; P-6 NON-DERIVED INPUT, 0.287 at identical closures, PATH-DEPENDENT minimal seeds) and in-access data cannot fix out-of-access physics (P-5, 1.547); the formalization records K_R = K_R(M, a) with partition dependence explicit (GRUT_FORMALIZATION_02.md:63). But in the Gaussian/free classes where it was tested, K is state-independent (D-1 Delta_K = 0.0 ground vs thermal; FRW SL(2,R) invariance 1.1e-13); the state enters through N (1.09) and, beyond Gaussianity, the record says only that the label becomes 'the full influence functional — never K alone' (DISCRIMINATOR_VERDICT_01.md:39-40); K's own state dependence outside those classes is not separately tested. Nothing in closure selects the law, state, coupling or seed. (b) No: matched (K,N) with different kappa_4 separate by 0.338; matching through order 2n leaves order-(2n+2) discrimination (17.2, 54.9; order-8 frozen gate red at 67.8 with post-hoc 325->320->276); the same ladder recurs at seed level (P-6, 54.9) and in EQ-1 (4.035, 16.65). Full-hierarchy matching certifies only in-class by cited bounded-moment determinacy, and the 'no untracked pair constructible' claim was never executed (P4_HIERARCHY_CORRECTION_01.md:45-49,83-85). In the converse direction, FS-1 shows that for conserved couplings in a free sector dynamic influence data of every order are identically blind (J(omega!=0) = 0) and only static multi-temperature or geometric access distinguishes (FS1_FREE_VERDICT_01.md:42-62). (c) No: the scalar floor (state-positivity check), the matrix cone (Gram reconstruction 5.6e-17), the full Gram hierarchy (vacuum on the face at 0.0e+00), the access algebra (bicommutant; central decomposition in P-6) and the off-stationary admissibility (Gram PSD 8.6e-14) all reduce to positivity of the global state restricted through the declared coupling; CP is dilation-guaranteed; R2 is graded '[IDENTITY] + [DERIVED-IN-CLASS]' (GRUT_FORMALIZATION_02.md:64); hbar is the floor height and is supplied; owner-binding: 'The cone is geometry of physically realizable influence data, not an independent explanation of quantum mechanics.' The one place positivity forces something on the gravity side — conservation of the probe coupling — it does so only given a supplied massless gauge-field probe (CC1_CCONS_VERDICT_01.md:15-17). (d) Per-verdict classes are tabulated in the ledger file §6: every positive result is DERIVED-IN-CLASS or [IDENTITY] in a finite declared class; every selector attack on the hierarchy (EQ-1, S4-1, FS-1, CC-1 constant level, P-6) returned NULL or reduced to a supplied input; the kernel on FRW is a recomputation from admitted inputs with state/KMS/stationary reduction supplied.

**Closure verdict.** Nothing additional was forced in this sector's stated classes. The computation that shows it: (1) every admissibility constraint found on influence data (J>=0, nu>=hbar J/2, nu+-J/2>=0, complete Gram positivity) was exhibited as the restriction of a positive global state through the declared coupling (P-2 verdict :22-27; P-3 Gram reconstruction 5.6e-17; P-4 vacuum 0.0e+00 / thermal 0.6878; C1-a Gram 8.6e-14 off the stationary domain). The redundancy rule was made binding from P-3 onward (P3_NC_LIFT_VERDICT_01.md:27-30; P4_HIERARCHY_CHARTER_01.md:8-10); P-2 itself is CONE-CONFIRMED / DERIVED-IN-CLASS given hbar, and its reduction to state positivity is demonstrated by P-4's two-point face, so that P-5 reads the 'NULL-AS-NEW-PRINCIPLE pattern of P-2/P-3/P-4' (P5_ACCESS_VERDICT_01.md:57-59) — known quantum mechanics in influence coordinates. (2) Closure with a declared subsystem leaves the complement free: at fixed (K,N) non-isomorphic worlds are S-indistinguishable to 4.5e-15 (D-1); at an identical in-access full hierarchy the out-of-access physics differs by 1.547 and appears in-access (0.38) only when the access boundary moves (P-5); identical dynamical closures hide physically different seeds (0.287) and minimal sufficient seeds are path-dependent (P-6). Owner-binding wording: 'the access boundary is currently an unresolved structural input'; 'access is arbitrary bookkeeping' FALSIFIED in-class; 'access is dynamically selected' NOT established; after P-6 'access seed = irreducible input, not a new law' (P6_SEED_SELECTION_CHARTER_01.md:3-9; G1_GEOMETRY_CHARTER_01.md:4-5). The P-5 instrument's phrase 'a physical boundary condition' is not owner wording. (3) The kernel is a recomputation from the supplied global law and background (FRW: A4+A7); what is missing on an evolving background is supplied structure (state, KMS, stationary reduction), not a new law; stationarity is a state property. (4) A nonstationary microscopic law yields a two-time kernel requiring a two-time datum (C1-a/C1-a2, in-class); the single spectral measure is a special case, not forced; the datum's minimality is unproved. (5) The hierarchy does not select the gravity-side coupling: c_full NULL in EQ-1, S4-1, FS-1 and at CC-1's constant level; what selects is supplied (Sel-4; C_cons reduced to a massless gauge-field probe; GeoInv). Surviving descriptive dividends, not promoted: one Gram condition replaces the cone's two axioms as low-order faces; the cumulant ladder's lambda-scaling on constructed pairs; the in-class elimination of the classical relaxational carrier. The physically load-bearing finding is the access seed under boundary change (P-5) and its non-selectability by any tested principle beyond block decomposition (P-6), with entropy/complexity/least-action/simplicity selectors not evaluated and the result 'NOT generalized to QFT/type-III': the subsystem's own influence data do not fix 'the rest', and no principle in the record fixes it.

**Classification.** `mixed` — Dominantly recovered_known_physics: positivity constraints are state positivity (P-2/P-3/P-4/P-5/P-6/C1-a); the influence functional is computed by Feynman-Vernon, not postulated; the FRW kernel is a free-field recomputation; P-2 is borrowed-standard Gaussian realizability mathematics. Conditional constraints in finite declared classes: the kappa_4 class split and cumulant ladder on constructed pairs; the Gaussian cone given hbar; the two-time datum at finite stepped level; R = 0.516 on the frozen grid; finite-order pair data cannot decide Sel-4 compliance; FS-1's identically blind dynamic data for conserved couplings (given C_cons); CC-1's positivity => conservation only for a massless gauge-field probe. Supplied model choices: hbar; the access seed and coupling operators (P-5/P-6); which hierarchy point per sector (amplitude, state, boundary data); FRW state, KMS/temperature and stationary reduction; Sel-4; C_cons as probe field content (CC-1, governing over the earlier 'unresolved import'); GeoInv. Rejected mechanisms: classical relaxational carrier (within the supplied quantum class, CA-1; escape open); every local dS->FRW substitution rule (34-130%); Dt-only packaging at finite stepped level (C1-a2); minimality/dynamical closure as a seed selector (P-6); initial uncertainty as a substitute for ongoing nonlinear noise (S2-1). Unexecuted tasks: the P-4 descriptiveness search (never run); P-6 prohibited selectors; C1-b and C1-c; minimality of the mixing datum; the smeared worldline nonstationarity object; a physically constrained bath for the C-B process (new-parent rescue, not authorized); S4-1 small-g robustness; FS-1 non-quadratic charges; the type-III generalization (map, not claims). No conjecture is promoted; 'the fundamental object is the cumulant hierarchy' stays a CANDIDATE.

**Remaining freedom.**
- Which hierarchy point reality realizes per sector: amplitude, state, boundary data (P4_HIERARCHY_VERDICT_01.md:72-76; SYN1_OWNER_RULING_01.md:63-66).
- The value of hbar, the floor height — located, not generated (P2_S1_OWNER_RULING_01.md:37-43).
- The access seed — selected by dynamics only up to block decomposition; beyond that 'interface-relative, order-relative, path-dependent, degeneracy-ridden' — and all out-of-access structure at fixed in-access hierarchy (P6_SEED_SELECTION_VERDICT_01.md:105-117; P5_ACCESS_VERDICT_01.md:77-85); entropy/complexity/least-action/simplicity selectors not evaluated (P6 :110-112).
- The full influence hierarchy beyond any finite matched order, and whether a matched-full-hierarchy pair with untracked physics exists — unsearched (P4_HIERARCHY_VERDICT_01.md:43-48; P4_HIERARCHY_CORRECTION_01.md:83-85).

### s03_level0_floor — Level-0 floor and earned core theorems (access rank-drop, DORD, DHERM, termination)  (`s03_level0_floor`)

**Question asked.** Do the Level-0 formulability-floor theorems (O-1 … O-7), the access rank-drop theorems, the D-ORD theorems and the floor termination condition constrain a closed whole, whose every subsystem's bath is the rest of the universe, beyond standard quantum theory — or are they theorems about supplied classes (access structure, generator/kernel families)? The floor's own recorded question is 'Can the system even be formulated without already assuming ordering, a generator, and determinism?' (L0_1_FLOOR_DESIGN_01.md:26-27), scoped to 'the linear relaxational class ẋ = −Kx on a finite substrate … Unitary quantum generators (H Hermitian) are a different object; … deferred, not designed here' (L0_1_FLOOR_DESIGN_01.md:15-20). Per-obligation questions: O-1 (L0_1D_CHARTER_01.md:21-23), O-5 (L0_1F_DORD_THEOREM_01.md:22-24), O-6 (L0_1G_CHARTER_01.md:20-24), O-7 (L0_1_FLOOR_O7_ADJUDICATION_01.md:78-80), rank-drop (L0_ACCESS_RANKDROP_THEOREM_01.md:18-19).

**Assumptions / model class.**
- Checkout: working tree a6be0e4, five WIP commits past the handoff commit 96a7e7e; git diff --stat 96a7e7e HEAD over every cited sector file is empty, so line numbers hold at 96a7e7e.
- Common substrate: K_b, the sealed C1 bath, (1:,1:) block of build_K(24,0), 23×23 symmetric irreducible tridiagonal positive definite, K = 0.3I + path Laplacian, λ_k = 2.3 − 2cos((2k−1)π/47), λ₁ ≈ 0.30447 (L0_1E_THEOREM_01.md:18-20, 43-48); retained observable is site 1, an end of the path (L0_ACCESS_RANKDROP_THEOREM_01.md:47-49).
- O-1 class: ring K_s = K_b + (e₁ − e₂₃)(e₁ − e₂₃)ᵀ; deformation K_{i,i+1} = −(1 − γa_i), K_{i+1,i} = −(1 + γa_i); C(γ) with 𝒜 = 23·ln((1+γ)/(1−γ)), γ_C ∈ {0, 0.1, 0.3, 0.6, 0.9}; B(γ) with 𝒜 = 0, γ_B ∈ {0.3, 0.9}; T(γ), γ_T ∈ {0, 0.9}; K_s held so accretivity holds by construction; every member Metzler; pre-stated theorem: affinity is necessary for a CM breach (L0_1D_CHARTER_01.md:32-36, 56-58, 69-88).
- O-2 class: attached one-way ring K(d,g) = d·I + a·E₁₁ − g·Q, not derived from K_b; n = 23, a = 1, g ∈ {1/20, 1/10, 1/4, 1/2, 1, 2, 4, 10}, exact fractions (L0_1H_DHERM_B_DESIGN_01.md:30-38; L0_1H_THEOREM_01.md:90-92). Theorem hypotheses: J-4 needs n odd and n ≥ 4 (DESIGN:65-75); P-1 needs −K Metzler irreducible and a stable member d > w* (DESIGN:82-89); P-2 needs n ≥ 3, a > 0 (DESIGN:98-106); J-6 needs P = gQ + a(I − E₁₁) ≥ 0 irreducible aperiodic (THEOREM:48-71). H-HERM-2 split into H2-m/H2-s/H2-n before evaluation (DESIGN:23-26, 139-143); mapping confirmed before the freeze (L0_1H_OWNER_RULING_02.md:8-9).
- O-3/O-4 class: OU dx = −Kx dt + B dW, Q = 2·diag(T_i), T_i declared rationals 'never constructed from K and never set from rung2's KMS lock' (L0_1E_THEOREM_01.md:21-23); T-0 needs only linear drift, additive noise, −K Hurwitz (:34-41); scope clause on every O-4 line (:153-156).
- O-5 classes: 𝒞₁ ẋ = −Kx with −K Hurwitz (pin = 0 and negative-spring members excluded by name); 𝒞₂ gradient convex quartic; 𝒞₃ stationary linear-Gaussian with all variables even under time reversal; conservative classes out of scope (L0_1F_DORD_THEOREM_01.md:42-62). D-3 needs f locally Lipschitz, V ∈ C¹, V̇ < 0 off equilibria (:115-117); D-4 needs a continuous flow and α(γ)∩ω(γ) ≠ ∅ (:166-170); D-5 needs OU stationarity and even variables (:217-218, 244-246). **Answer.** Every theorem in this sector is a theorem about a supplied class, and none is formulated on a quantum state space. (1) The floor's E1–E4 edges, O-1 (affinity vs asymmetry on the K_b-derived 23-site ring), O-2 (accretivity vs stability on the separate one-way ring K(d,g), n = 23), O-3/O-4 (OU with declared diagonal noise on K_b) and O-5 (static presentations, Lyapunov order, lag distinguishability for 𝒞₁/𝒞₂/𝒞₃) all presuppose a declared first-order dissipative generator, a declared end-site readout and a declared noise rule; the design places unitary quantum generators outside the floor (L0_1_FLOOR_DESIGN_01.md:15-20); the generator is 'a presupposition on this document's face' (L0_1F_DORD_THEOREM_01.md:34-35), later ruled IRREDUCIBLE/SUPPLIED (S5_OWNER_RULING_03.md:99-100). (2) Rank-drop Theorem 1 is identity-grade at the declared readout (det DΦ = ∏ w_k^{N−k} = 1 for K_b), ruled TRIVIAL/IDENTITY and not to be generalized; Theorem 2 is probe-dependent, 'not ontology' (L0_ACCESS_BRIDGE_OWNER_RULING_02.md:13-14, 57-71). (3) The one obligation that tests a closed whole with an internal bath, O-6 (classical conservative harmonic chain + partition + product preparation), returns a non-forcing result: strict pointwise ordering is FALSIFIED by band-edge memory (ruled from identities plus a non-member asymptotic check; the charter was never frozen or run), the past hypothesis gives an anti-arrow at t = 0⁺, and the later S6-1 net arrow (N = ∞ primary, N = 95 non-adjudicating cross-check) is integrated, ensemble and preparation-relative (L0_1G_OWNER_RULING_02.md:9-41; S6_OWNER_RULING_02.md:100-102, 120-121; S6_1_VERDICT_01.md:95-98). (4) O-7 shows the floor's own two-property reduction fails and what survives is a list of non-implications inside declared classes (L0_1_FLOOR_O7_OWNER_RULING_01.md:10-35). (5) The termination condition 'executes nothing' (L0_1_FLOOR_TERMINATION_DRAFT_01.md:82-85); it is a stopping rule, not a physical statement. Therefore no theorem in this sector constrains a closed whole beyond standard quantum theory; what the sector recovered is standard classical dynamical-systems, OU and harmonic-chain mathematics, certified exactly on a small set of declared families (K_b, its ring, the one-way ring, the pinned chain), plus the explicit record that the generator, readout, noise origin, preparation and physical lift remain supplied.

**Closure verdict.** Nothing additional was forced in the stated classes. The computation that shows it is the floor's own record: (a) D-4 — on any orbit with α(γ)∩ω(γ) ≠ ∅ no continuous state function is strictly monotone; every orbit is recurrent for positive-definite quadratic Hamiltonians and finite-dimensional unitary dynamics, 'Not every conservative system is recurrent (a free particle is not), so the statement is scoped' (L0_1F_DORD_THEOREM_01.md:166-175, 197-202); (b) O-6 — in the conservative pinned chain with partition and uncorrelated product start, strict ordering of heat current and relative entropy fails (J bounded by t⁻³; D, Ḋ leading t⁻⁶; non-member check J < 0 on 49.6% of the late window, 739 sign changes) and the past hypothesis ≠ arrow at t = 0 (L0_1G_OWNER_RULING_02.md:9-38; L0_1G_CORRECTIONS_01.md:18-24; L0_1G_PREFREEZE_REVIEW_01.md:35-39); what survives is an integrated, preparation-relative net direction on the infinite chain (S6_OWNER_RULING_02.md:100-102, 120-121); (c) P-1 — retained-site response on the one-way ring cannot see state-norm growth, an observability identity (L0_1H_DHERM_B_DESIGN_01.md:82-95); (d) the generator is presupposed and later ruled IRREDUCIBLE/SUPPLIED (L0_1F_DORD_THEOREM_01.md:29-35; S5_OWNER_RULING_03.md:99-100); (e) the readout is declared and observability at it is an identity (L0_ACCESS_BRIDGE_OWNER_RULING_02.md:13, 59-60); (f) no earned structure selects the physical lift (L0_ACCESS_BRIDGE_OWNER_RULING_02.md:30; L0_POSTFLOOR_DEPOSIT_01.md:23). Of the floor's own hypotheses, H-HERM-2, strict H-ORD and the two-property reduction failed; the generator, access structure and lift were post-floor gates that returned SUPPLIED. The sector recovered known mathematics and classical physics only; the floor 'makes no claim about the axioms of reality' (L0_1_FLOOR_DEPOSIT_01.md:142-143), and 'nothing forced' is 'not proof that no possible future extra postulate could have content' (NEXT_CHAT_HANDOFF.md:128-129).

**Classification.** `mixed` — Recovered known physics/mathematics: D-1/D-1′ (Laplace, Kronecker, Ho–Kalman, Lanczos), D-3 (strict Lyapunov ordering), D-4 (α∩ω recurrence obstruction; Conley cited), D-5 (OU reversibility ⟺ KΣ = ΣKᵀ), T-0/T-4 (Gaussian regression theorem, FDT member Σ = K⁻¹), T-2 (Bernstein), T-6 (cone from linearity in T), T-11 (OU detailed-balance break), P-1 (Perron bound on Metzler generators), J-4, O-6's band-edge memory and I-5 slip, S-6 return to equilibrium (Riemann–Lebesgue on an a.c. spectrum). Conditional constraint (declared class only): E1–E4 edges; O-1 CLASS-SPLIT; O-2 nesting w* < d_acc < d_mono at 8 g; O-4 cone location (R* ≈ 83.16, ≈ 9800, ∞; 22 steps pass); Theorem 1 at the earned readout; S-6 net arrow (preparation-relative, N = ∞). Supplied model choice: generator and orientation (A-3/A-4), readout/access seed (A-13), the declared noise rule and its physical origin (A-11), the ring/one-way-ring families, O-6's preparation and partition (A-14), the lift (A-5). Rejected mechanism: H-HERM-2 (O-2), strict H-ORD (O-6), the two-property reduction (O-7), H-PROJ as cross-program explanation (forest layer). Unexecuted task: the O-6 chartered strict-gate run on N ∈ {23, 47, 95} and the K-3 drawdown map (ARBITRARY); K_b interior-site rank-drop sets; S-4, S-7, S-8 not selected; faithful-representation lift audit preserved, not opened. Conjecture (fenced by the owner): linearity as an 'effective mathematical regime' ('suggests'); the ring-lap mechanism for d_mono.

**Remaining freedom.**
- The temporal generator kind and its sign/orientation remain supplied (S5_OWNER_RULING_03.md:99-100; L0_1F_DORD_THEOREM_01.md:256-260; S5_OWNER_RULING_01.md:54-57).
- The access seed / readout interface; multi-site access sets are 'declared … that the L0 records do not earn' (L0_ACCESS_BRIDGE_CORRECTIONS_01.md:10-12; LEVEL0_FOREST_SYNTHESIS_01.md:49-54, 144).
- The physical origin of the noise: NO-PHYSICAL-BATH-CANDIDATE = TRUE; S2-HB is a representation theorem, not an origin theorem (S2_HB_OWNER_RULING_02.md:9-18, 47-48); S-1 unresolved at the physical-noise-origin level (GRUT_SUCCESSOR_STATUS_01.md:18).
- The physical lift and its prices: statistics, complex/symplectic doubling, dilation/bath preparation, readout identification, superselection, domain, physical ħ (L0_POSTFLOOR_DEPOSIT_01.md:23; L0_LIFT_SELECTION_OWNER_RULING_02.md:54-57).
- Preparation, partition and coarse-graining for any arrow; the S-6 arrow is preparation-relative and coarse-graining is not derived (S6_OWNER_RULING_02.md:116-121).
- What sets d_mono; dependence on n, a, retained site; whether threshold ordering can reverse in other classes (S-7, L0_1_FLOOR_SUCCESSOR_LIST.md:23; not selected, GRUT_SUCCESSOR_STATUS_01.md:24).
- Interior-site rank-drop sets of K_b, not established (L0_ACCESS_RANKDROP_THEOREM_01.md:131-133; L0_POSTFLOOR_DEPOSIT_01.md:62-63).
- The pin-free locality fork, held outside the floor and undecided (L0_1_FLOOR_DEPOSIT_01.md:128; GRUT_SUCCESSOR_STATUS_01.md:35).
- Finite-N behaviour of the O-6 strict gates on N ∈ {23, 47, 95} with the guard band (never run; L0_1G_CHARTER_01.md:157-187); S-6 answered the coarse-arrow question at N = ∞ with N = 95 as a non-adjudicating cross-check and classified the drawdown map ARBITRARY (S6_1_COARSEGRAINED_ARROW_CHARTER_01.md:48; S6_COARSEGRAINED_ARROW_01.md:360-363).

**Failures retained.**
- O-2 = FALSIFIED, terminal (H2-m by theorem P-1; H2-s by exact counterexample at all 8 g); mechanism not established; ring-lap interpretation untested (L0_1H_OWNER_RULING_03.md:8-16, 35-39); the label was fixed by P-1 before any member evaluation (L0_1H_THEOREM_01.md:24-27; L0_1H_OWNER_RULING_01.md:42-44).
- O-2 design rev 1: '1 + a/g' typo (L0_1H_DHERM_B_DESIGN_01.md:54); 'Rev 1 wrongly gave δ = O(a/n)' (:113); E-1 'criterion was wrong' and ''g ≳ 0.5' was too high' (:117-129); '§4's overclaim was withdrawn' (:13-14, 157-160); one toy ran n = 25, a = 1, 'a close neighbour of the family' (:17-18); run incident: e¹¹⁰⁰ float overflow, isolated (L0_1H_OWNER_RULING_03.md:32-33).
- O-6 = FALSIFIED (strict pointwise / whole-window form) with NO chartered run: 'Nothing was computed on the declared pin-0.3 chain'; 'L0-1g is not frozen, not run, and banks nothing' (L0_1G_PREFREEZE_REVIEW_01.md:6-7, 112); magnitude maps not evaluated under the ruling (L0_1G_OWNER_RULING_02.md:39-41); calc/l01g_bath_arrow.py and L0_1G_RESULT.json absent from this checkout. ### s04_grut_I_II — GRUT I–II vacuum response (recovery with imports, projector choice, conditional exclusions, supplied arrow) — VERIFIED AND CORRECTED  (`s04_grut_I_II`)

**Question asked.** Closed-whole form as assigned: in the responsive-vacuum sector, what did treating the gravitational vacuum as an open system whose bath is the rest of the universe force beyond known physics — what was recovered only with imports, what was a projector choice, which exclusions are conditional, and is the arrow supplied? Retained: the campaign asked different questions — V1 'how far is the responsiveness bet forced to reach?' and V2 'What is universal about constitutive structure itself, before any microscopic ontology?' (GRUT_II_Agenda.md:3-7); no primary source poses 'bath = rest of the universe'; the bath is a declared KMS equilibrium bath ('borrowed standard + declared equilibrium', GRUT_PREDICTION_GATE_GAMMA_T.md:92) with open pole structure ('single pole vs branch cut ... The bath.', GRUT_I_What_Survived.md:55). The closed-whole reading is imposed by the 2026-10-19 handoff, itself 'a construction brief, not a newly executed general theorem' (NEXT_CHAT_HANDOFF.md:8-16, 107-109).

**Assumptions / model class.**
- Ontological bet, not derivation: the vacuum 'is a responsive open quantum system — a Schwinger–Keldysh influence action with a system↔bath split (rung1) ... a Bin-1 primitive ... not a derivation' (GRUT_I_What_Survived.md:13, 53). Live register (Ruling B split 2026-08-23): rung1_inin_formalism tier derived-pending, ledger_delta 4; rung1_ontology_finite_memory 'a STANCE, explicitly not derived', tier assumed, +1 (provenance/claims.json:5, 11, 28, 34).
- Equilibrium declared: FDT/KMS detailed balance 'as an admission gate' (rung2) (GRUT_I_What_Survived.md:14, 54); 'KMS lock + T_dS = H/2π — borrowed standard + declared equilibrium' (GRUT_PREDICTION_GATE_GAMMA_T.md:92); this gate 'is exactly the object where the direction enters (β>0)' (ARROW_OF_TIME.md:70). Live tier derived-pending, ledger_delta −1 ('N removed as an independent input ... T is NOT booked as an independent input anywhere, DELIBERATELY') (provenance/claims.json:47 node).
- Finite memory 'a posit within the rung1 ontology'; pole vs cut open, rung3 derived-pending (GRUT_I_What_Survived.md:15, 55).
- Pure-TT projector 'CHOSEN, not forced'; family K = c₂P^TT + c₀P⁰ˢ with c₀ = 0 'an input, not a consequence'; 'GRUT keeps one survivor and zeroes the other BY HAND (on the FRW frame mu_linear uses: 5 independent form factors, GRUT keeps 1, zeroes 4)' (GRUT_I_What_Survived.md:65; GRUT_II_What_Survived.md:43; provenance/claims.json:319).

- Arrow direction imported as the Past Hypothesis, 'a Bin-1 primitive'; arrow_of_time tier assumed, ledger_delta 1, 'NOT derived; LOCATED' (GRUT_I_What_Survived.md:63; provenance/claims.json:262 node, :281).

### s05_grut_III — GRUT III / observer bath (P0–P5, P1 identification, modular KMS, GR recovery, universal coupling) — verified and corrected  (`s05_grut_III`)

**Question asked.** Once "every subsystem's bath is the rest of the universe" is realised algebraically as P1 (medium = (A(O), ω|_{A(O)}, σ^ω_t); bath = commutant A(O)′, = A(O′) under Haag duality), do P0–P5 force any state, kernel, coupling, constant, arrow or outcome rule that GR + QFT in curved spacetime does not already contain? Sub-questions: is P1 an identification or a law; is modular KMS a temperature for a physical clock; what is imported in GR recovery and universal coupling; what do the GRUT III predictions depend on. (GRUT_III/README.md:13-16; NEXT_CHAT_HANDOFF.md:92-94)

**Assumptions / model class.**
- P0 (POSTULATE, +1): local relativistic QFT on a Lorentzian manifold, net of von Neumann algebras, Einstein causality, covariance, spectrum condition, Hadamard states; bundles Lorentzian signature, Hilbert structure and field content (01_PRINCIPLES.md:14-21).
- P1 (IDENTIFICATION, 0): medium = (A(O), ω|_{A(O)}, modular flow); bath = commutant; type III₁ algebras so no tensor factorisation, no reduced density matrix, no influence functional, no dynamics (01:23-31).
- P2 (POSTULATE, +1): S = ηA and Clausius across every local causal horizon; area law IMPORTED; η = 1/(4Għ) inserted (datum G); 'The Clausius relation is the one structural postulate' (01:45-52).
- P3 (POSTULATE, +1): vertex (κ/2)h_μν T^μν with κ² = 32πG inserted; universality = operational equivalence principle (01:54-59).
- P4 (POSTULATE, +1): future horizon traced out, β > 0 (Past Hypothesis, located not derived); P5 (POSTULATE, +1): non-contextuality → Gleason/Busch Born rule, IMPORTED (01:61-74).
- Data: ħ, c, G, k_B, Λ, particle spectrum; 'GRUT III does not derive G and says so' (01:10-12). Accounting: five framework postulates; P1 an identification (01:78-88; 04:8,26-33).
- Model class of every computed result: free conformal or massless minimally coupled scalar, Bunch–Davies/conformal vacuum, geodesic/comoving worldline, exact dS or power-law FRW with constant ε, two-point (Gaussian) level, fixed-mean-time (Wigner) spectra at ω ≠ 0 (02:259-266; 07:126-128; 11:126-128). D11 second order complete only under the full adiabatic hierarchy ε₃ = η̇/Hη small (11:125-126); massless velocity correlator IR-finite only for ε < 2/3 (RESULTS.md:668-669; 11:158-159).
- Checkout: HEAD a6be0e4 (WIP descendant of handoff 96a7e7e); sector sources byte-identical to 96a7e7e (git diff --stat empty).

**Answer.** (1) P1 is an identification, not a law: zero postulate credit, no dynamics, no influence functional, no type-III factorisation, and no state-selection condition; the restriction map exists for every global state, so P1 cannot fix even a local one-point function (01:23-43; 00:19-21; CLOSURE_SELECTION_RESPONSE.md:7-11,28-40). (2) Modular KMS is automatic for a faithful normal state and is a temperature for a physical clock only where the modular flow is geometric — each such case an imported theorem (Bisognano–Wichmann, Kay–Wald, Gibbons–Hawking, Hislop–Longo) (01:30-39; CSR:42-45). In FRW with ε ≠ 0 the apparent horizon is not Killing and P1's definition assigns the region no temperature (09:21-25; RESULTS:649-655); what is computable is conventional QFT: the comoving worldline's Wigner spectrum is KMS at (ä/ȧ)/2π to O(ε) in both conformal (v11) and massless (v14) sectors, a one-sided instantaneous rate differs at O(ε), T_KH is a separate near-horizon scaling-limit KMS temperature (KPV 2021, cosmological case open; tension with P1's geometric list unreconciled), T₀ = 1/(2πR_A) a geometric label, and the exact ΛCDM kernel today is KMS at no β (RESULTS:607-620,644-680,787-798; 02:194-196,213-221; 09:30-33; 13:163-167). (3) GR recovery imports the area law, the Jacobson 1995 derivation and Hadamard/Kay–Wald thermality and inserts η = 1/(4Għ) via the Clausius postulate P2; Λ is an integration constant; one-loop induced gravity with SM content gives Σk₁ = +1/6 or −1/2, so G is not derived in that class. Universal coupling inserts κ² = 32πG (P3); its derived content Im Π(s) = c_eff(s)Gs² with weights 1:3:12 is contained in Han–Lykken–Zhang 1999 (01:45-59; 02:60-69,80-99,199-209; 08:119-138; 13:156-162). (4) The GRUT III predictions depend on P0's field content and Hadamard states, the Bunch–Davies/conformal vacuum, the imported horizon-thermality theorems, the inserted G and κ, and (D11/D13) the slow-roll hypothesis in the Wigner convention at ω ≠ 0; F1–F6 are all shared with ΛCDM / GR + QFT and 'GRUT III makes no prediction that distinguishes it from GR + QFT in curved spacetime' (03:9,13-24); the framework-level falsifiers (03:40-44) test P0/P1, P2, P5 and D2, not a GRUT-specific claim. **Closure verdict.** Nothing additional was forced in the stated class (covariant QFT with Hadamard states; bath = commutant / causal complement; KMS states), and the sector contains the computations that show it. Realising 'bath = rest of the universe' as P1 forces only tautological KMS in the modular parameter (01:30-33); by the FDT identity N_f = ½coth(βω/2)ρ_f any extra noise at the same β is extra dissipation, i.e. a change of field content (03:5-9; 07:20-38). In every regime computed — exact dS (v8: KMS defect 2.41e-35, residual template EMPTY), quasi-dS conformal sector through O(ε²) (v11: D/ε → 2πω/H̄ to ≲1e-9), quasi-dS massless sector at O(ε) (v14: residual halving ratios → 4 only at H_eff), horizon first laws (v12: identities of the Einstein equations) — 'the answer is fixed by conventional theory and P0–P5 add no selection' (05:123-125). No state is selected (restriction exists for every state; the parameter-free SJ candidate violates P2 and the CMB; the DMP horizon state is unproven at ν = 3/2), no constant is fixed (G inserted, Λ an integration constant, Sakharov fails with SM content), and P4 (arrow) and P5 (Born) remain postulates with no computation proposed (05:27-40). 'before there is a geometry there is no bath — the universe has no outside' (13:61-62). What survives is GR + QFT with a thermodynamic reading, the Δρ residual instrument, and a priced ten-line constraint sheet for any sixth postulate (12:36-80). This is not a theorem that no future postulate could have content: the state door (N1) and the renormalisation-constant door (N2) are pre-registered and unexecuted (13:357-375), and the sector's own survey file carries an unreconciled reading-report verdict that the graviton infrared is 'a genuine, open window' (10:104) against the author's 'not a GRUT one' (10:138).

**Classification.** `mixed` — Recovered known physics: D1, D3, D4, D7, D8, D10 (form), D12 (IMPORTED, 02:7,60,71,107,111,138,199); D2 (DERIVED, 'corollary of standard results', 02:25); D5's weights and threshold laws (contained in HLZ, 'a verification, not a discovery', 08:126-127); D9; T1–T6 ('all imported/standard', 07:40); the v11/v14/v12/v13 computations ('conventional QFT', 02:265-266; 12:15-25). Conditional constraints: D11 first-order thermality (ε, η first-order small; Wigner convention; ω ≫ εH; second order under the full adiabatic hierarchy; not today) (02:160-170; 11:125-126); D13 (constant ε, comoving worldline, ω ≠ 0, massless scalar, ε < 2/3 for the velocity correlator; general slow roll and gravitons not established) (02:259-266); D5's scope (√s variable; illustrative staircase; massive-vector polynomial not computed) (02:93-97); F1–F6 as falsifiers of the minimal framework only (03:13-15); CP-05's η < 1/3 within its kinetic family (CP05:125). Supplied model choices: P0's content and data; η = 1/(4Għ); κ² = 32πG; P4; P5; the Bunch–Davies/Hadamard state; the fixed-mean-time convention ('a choice, but not a free parameter', 02:242-243). Rejected mechanisms: a₀ via Jacobson; memory time 1/(4H); bulk Planckian fluid; c_eff at LIGO frequencies; Born via HJW; w ≡ −1 as discriminator; '+16 → +5 compression'/'GR hosted'/'frontier dissolved' (03:53-63); v11 rev. 2 coefficient 1.01; v13 rev. 1 inference; the record's rung-3 and S5-1 kernels (SUPPLIED); the sharp Sorkin–Johnston state; the closure fork's D2 novelty, sign-only curvature falsifier and all-sequestering-positive-curvature claim (CSR:164-167). Conjecture: P1 selecting the DMP horizon state (13:281-286); a non-inserted residual in the interacting gravitational infrared (07:131-135; 10:104); N1, N2 (13:359-368). Unexecuted tasks: O7 items 1, 2, 4 (05:143-155); literature check for r(ω) and −(1 + 2 ln 2) and first-principles derivation of the v13 13.5 invariant (12:6-8; 13:374-375; 11:176); general slow-roll and tensor extensions of D13; O5 item 2; SJ region ending today; N1–N4; independent review of CP-04/CP-05; closure fork B Step 1 (CSR:172-174); reading Brandenberger–Kahn 1982, Abbott–Wise 1984, Lucchin–Matarrese 1985 (10:110-113,167).

**Remaining freedom.**

### s06_cp12_geometry — CP-12 geometry / preparation (geometry-selection obstructions, min-entropy extension)  (`s06_cp12_geometry`)

**Question asked.** Given a closed system (finite labelled frozen CP-12 under closed unitary evolution, each subsystem's bath being the rest of the graph/matter/rotor system), does closure force an emergent spatial geometry or any geometry-selection law beyond known physics? Specifically: (a) can a loop-rich microscopic graph phase be assembled from the supplied preparations, and what initial resource does that require (cp12_preparation_minentropy_audit/REPORT.md:1-3,92,106; parent cp12_local_circulation_resource/REPORT.md:94-132); (b) can CP-12's connection sector or matter response select or propagate geometry locally (14_CP12:30-37,50-55); (c) in the earlier Gaussian-network class (G1/GS1), do influence/access data alone reconstruct or select geometry?

**Assumptions / model class.**
- Frozen CP-12: finite labelled graph, N vertices, Q present edges, fermion hopping, positive rewiring Laplacian incl. diagonal, U(1) rotors with unbounded integer electric fields, exact Gauss constraints (cp12_preparation_minentropy_audit/REPORT.md:12; cp12_qn_degree2_ground_exclusion/REPORT.md:3)
- Frozen couplings (kappa,m,t,epsilon_E,Gamma)=(1/2,1,1,1/4,1/10) (cp12_qn_degree2_ground_exclusion/REPORT.md:3; cp12_local_circulation_resource/DERIVATION.md:23); checker hard-codes eps=.25, hop=1., gamma=.1 (check.py:39)
- External time parameter, Hamiltonian bounded below: 'Every codex model (CP-11, CP-12, RG01, RG02) evolves in an external parameter with a Hamiltonian bounded below' (GRUT_III/13_SYNTHESIS.md:137); closed unitary evolution rho_t=e^{-itH}rho_0e^{itH} (REPORT.md:12)
- Labelled counting; no permutation-gauge quotient (REPORT.md:3,103; cp12_local_circulation_resource/DERIVATION.md:10-14; 13_SYNTHESIS.md:111-113 for the template theorem)
- Min-entropy extension: fixed density Q=nu N+O(1); target S = at least ceil(alpha N) vertex-disjoint cycles of microscopic length <=R, R,alpha,nu fixed positive constants; Pi_S graph-diagonal projector (REPORT.md:3,12,113)
- Main theorem preparation: flat incoherent neutral-sea mixture rho_0=(1/D) sum_G |G,0><G,0|, D=binom(M,Q), M=binom(N,2) (REPORT.md:7-12)
- General variant: (i) ||rho_0||_inf <= e^{cN}/binom(M,Q) AND (ii) <H-kappa Q>_0 = O(N) (REPORT.md:86-90); arbitrary-spectrum form uses H_inf(rho_0) with energy condition <H-kappa Q> <= cN called 'essential for the electric-field Markov tail' (REPORT.md:113-119)
- Proof ingredients: unitary invariance of operator norm, Markov's inequality on F, energy conservation with m>=0, R>=0, T_hop>=-2|t_hop|Q, parent's labelled cycle counting (REPORT.md:31-52,64-68,104)
- Explicitly outside the min-entropy assumption: pure uniform coherent graph state and single pure graph state (REPORT.md:90,101); permutation-gauge quotient, open-system cooling, postselection, infinite-temperature thermodynamics, higher-density ground state (REPORT.md:103,159)
- Parent (63bbd4af) class: complete quantum Gibbs state at every fixed positive temperature, full labelled Hilbert space, every fixed nu>0 (cp12_local_circulation_resource/REPORT.md:76-87; DERIVATION.md:54); all-time variant needs D(rho_0||sigma_beta)=O(N) and 'assumes closed evolution without postselection' (REPORT.md:104-114); does not decide the Q=2N or Q=3N ground-state phase (REPORT.md:148)
- Deficit Delta H_inf = log2 D_N - H_inf is relative to the neutral labelled graph subspace, not an absolute maximally mixed state; can be negative (REPORT.md:138-142; cp12_energy_matched_preparation/REPORT.md:21)
- G1/GS1: small quadratic (Gaussian) static networks, supplied access structure, no gravity input (GS1:86-88,27-29; G1:105-110) — a different model class from CP-12, correctly not cited in the CP-12 handoff row

**Answer.** All obstructions in this sector are model- and preparation-specific, each carrying a declared model (frozen labelled CP-12 at fixed couplings and Q/N) and a declared state class: the min-entropy no-go (flat incoherent mixture, or any state with ||rho_0||_inf <= e^{cN}/D AND O(N) excess energy, labelled counting, a specific loop-packing projector; REPORT.md:3,12,86-90,102-103); its parent relative-entropy no-go (D(rho_0||sigma_beta)=O(N), closed evolution without postselection, every fixed nu>0, constants 'too loose for useful small-N probability bounds', partner audit outstanding; cp12_local_circulation_resource/REPORT.md:104-114,152,162); the earlier template thermal exclusion (nu>1, vacuous below N≈10^7–10^8 at beta=1, nothing at nu=1; 13_SYNTHESIS.md:76-79,301-302 — a different theorem from the parent, graded two days before it); the S_N marginal theorem (permutation-invariant preparations, unconditional labelled marginals; cp12_exchangeability_selection/REPORT.md:7-23); holonomy Theorem A (Q=N, every state) and the degree-two ground exclusion (Q=N, unaudited proof candidate; cp12_holonomy_locality_gate/REPORT.md:17-37,71-78; cp12_qn_degree2_ground_exclusion/REPORT.md:3,7-13); matter-blind connection creation (uniform -Gamma/M; 14_CP12:30-37); the finite-size diameter trap (Q > binom(N-1,2); cp12_unmasked_formation/REPORT.md:19-27); and G1/GS1 (quadratic networks, supplied access; GS1:86-88; G1:105-110). The exact assumption class of the min-entropy extension is a min-entropy-plus-energy class, not a coherence class: (i) largest eigenvalue <= e^{cN}/binom(M,Q) (or any H_inf entering the sharpened bound) together with (ii) <H-kappa Q>_0 <= cN, the energy condition being 'essential' (REPORT.md:86-90,113-119); the handoff row's 'particular incoherent high-entropy class' names the main theorem's preparation correctly (REPORT.md:5,25,159) and omits the energy condition and general variant. Pure coherent preparations are confirmed outside it: every pure state has H_inf=0; a pure uniform coherent graph state and a single pure graph state fail (i) (REPORT.md:90), the theorem 'does not apply to the pure coherent neutral sea' (REPORT.md:101), and the coherent superposition 'escapes this necessary-condition obstruction, without any proof that it assembles geometry... coherence is not uniquely required' (REPORT.md:157); the parent says the same of its relative-entropy version (cp12_local_circulation_resource/REPORT.md:125-127). No theorem excludes every emergent geometry: the min-entropy theorem does not prohibit nonlocal, coarse-grained, relational, or growing-cycle-length space and makes no claim about gauge quotients, cooling, postselection or the higher-density ground state (REPORT.md:100,103); the parent 'do[es] not decide the Q=2N or Q=3N ground-state phase' (cp12_local_circulation_resource/REPORT.md:148-151); the S_N theorem does not forbid symmetric superpositions, infinite-volume SSB, or record-conditioned order (exchangeability:23); Theorem A does not exclude effective loop observables via superpositions or coarse-graining (holonomy:39); the degree-two exclusion is not an exclusion of all CP-12 phases (qn_degree2:13); the six-site propagation failure is 'not a no-go theorem for every CP-12 phase' (source_resolved:11-12). Conversely no packet claims a formed, persistent, dimension-selected spatial phase; the strongest positives are finite-N correlations, finite responses and prepared-host transport (relational_record_gate:5; unfiltered_record_gate:7; active_host:3-5; local_circulation_resource:53-54).

**Closure verdict.** Nothing additional was forced beyond known physics in the stated class, and the computations showing it are the parent relative-entropy theorem and its min-entropy sharpening. Closure does work at two steps of the min-entropy proof: unitary invariance of the density-matrix spectrum, ||rho_t||_inf = ||rho_0||_inf for all t (REPORT.md:31-35), and conservation of the extensive energy of a time-independent Hamiltonian, which bounds the electric tail by Markov's inequality (REPORT.md:37-52,132; the energy condition is 'essential', REPORT.md:119). In the parent it is the same pair, as conservation of entropy and <H> giving D(rho_T||sigma_beta)=D(rho_0||sigma_beta)+beta(<V>_0-<V>_T) (cp12_local_circulation_resource/REPORT.md:96-102; DERIVATION.md:310-320). With labelled cycle counting these convert 'does frozen CP-12 assemble a microscopic loop-rich graph?' into 'which supplied initial state carries at least alpha N log2 N bits of min-entropy deficit relative to the flat neutral-graph mixture, with O(N) excess energy?' (REPORT.md:150-155), or in the parent's form D(rho_0||sigma_beta) >= p_* alpha N log N - O(N) (cp12_local_circulation_resource/REPORT.md:129-130). That is an initial-state obligation, not a state-selection law, as the source says three times (REPORT.md:104,111,159), the parent says ('identifies a necessary resource ... does not supply the missing spatial phase', REPORT.md:157-158) and the partner audit confirms ('necessary resource... does not make low min-entropy sufficient', cp12_energy_matched_preparation/REPORT.md:21). What fixes the initial state remains supplied (cp12_unmasked_formation/REPORT.md:9), consistent with the handoff's own list of what closure does not force (NEXT_CHAT_HANDOFF.md:98-100). The explicit min-entropy bound is vacuous at every size its checker prints, through N=10^18 (REPORT.md:96; run output loglow/(NlogN)=+0.206365 at nu=2, +0.251441 at nu=3), and the parent's constants are 'too loose for useful small-N probability bounds' (cp12_local_circulation_resource/REPORT.md:152). The parent's proof has no independent partner audit (REPORT.md:162). Everything else here is a model-specific conditional theorem, an unaudited proof candidate, a rejected mechanism, or recovered known physics (GS1:78-80; G1:38-39; 13_SYNTHESIS.md:84; cp12_local_circulation_resource/REPORT.md:160).

**Classification.** `mixed` — Conditional constraint: min-entropy/energy all-time bound and alpha N log2 N deficit threshold (REPORT.md:14-23,86-90,122-155); parent K_R relative-entropy bounds, UNAUDITED (cp12_local_circulation_resource/REPORT.md:76-87,104-114,162); template thermal exclusion for nu>1 as graded (13_SYNTHESIS.md:301-302); S_N marginal uniformity (exchangeability:13-20); unicyclic Lambda_R<=R/N (holonomy:11-37); extensive-circulation bounds (extensive_circulation:11-27); access-class split in Gaussian networks (GS1:15-29). Conjecture / unaudited proof candidate: Q=N degree-two ground exclusion (qn_degree2:3; holonomy:71-78); Penrose/past-hypothesis resemblance (REPORT.md:159); relational geometry need not choose labels (exchangeability:23). Recovered known physics: proof ingredients (REPORT.md:35,104; local_circulation_resource:160); counting obstruction prior art (13_SYNTHESIS.md:84-90); G1/GS1 machinery (GS1:78-80; G1:38-39). Supplied model choice: frozen Hamiltonian and couplings, Q/N sector, neutral ray / phase mask / recorded host / prepared torus, labelled counting (qn_degree2:3; energy_matched:9; unmasked_formation:9; local_circulation_resource:36-37,53-54; 13_SYNTHESIS.md:111-113). Rejected mechanism: connection sector as local geometry source (14_CP12:30-37); recorded-ring metric (source_resolved:3-8); outside-core hopping suppression (dynamical_suppression:3-8); LocPos as selector (GS1:58-70); arrival-time functionals as metric (G1:60-77). Unexecuted task: Next Work test at Q=3N (REPORT.md:106; local_circulation_resource:154-156); parent partner audit (local_circulation_resource:162); tensor-charter amendments (14_CP12:96-119); five-step relational gate (exchangeability:27-33); G1 second attack gated on owner ruling (G1:119-124).

**Remaining freedom.**
- The initial state: any preparation with sufficient min-entropy (or relative-entropy) deficit and O(N) energy evades the no-go; nothing in the sector selects one (REPORT.md:157,159; cp12_local_circulation_resource/REPORT.md:125-127; cp12_energy_matched_preparation/REPORT.md:9)
- Density sector nu=Q/N and counting convention: for the template theorem 'nu = 1 is open' closes only in unlabelled counting (13_SYNTHESIS.md:111-113); the K_R and min-entropy theorems hold for every fixed nu>0 but leave the Q=2N/Q=3N ground-state phase undecided (cp12_local_circulation_resource/REPORT.md:148) and the higher-density ground state unaddressed (REPORT.md:103)
- Nonlocal, coarse-grained, relational, or growing-cycle-length geometries (REPORT.md:100; cp12_local_circulation_resource/REPORT.md:149-151); graph superpositions and matter-correlation loop observables (cp12_holonomy_locality_gate/REPORT.md:39); symmetric superpositions, infinite-volume SSB, record-conditioned order (cp12_exchangeability_selection/REPORT.md:23)
- Open-system cooling, external entropy removal, postselection, and an initial-condition theory, all outside the closed-unitary no-go (REPORT.md:103,159; cp12_local_circulation_resource/REPORT.md:114,149)
- Non-Gaussian sectors for influence-data geometry selection (GS1:86-88); better-conditioned instruments for G1 (G1:119-124)
- Weinberg–Witten escape route and source of kinematic nonlocality for energy, to be declared before any tensor pole is computed (14_CP12:57-70,111-115)

**Failures retained.**
- Min-entropy bound vacuous at small N and 'may become nonvacuous only at astronomically large N' (REPORT.md:96); confirmed by this verification's checker: loglow/(NlogN) = +0.206365 (nu=2) and +0.251441 (nu=3) at N=10^18, high_tail_upper 1.120316 (nu=3); finite-size bounds 'extremely loose' (REPORT.md:27)
- Theorem does not improve the full D(rho_0||sigma_beta)=O(N) class (REPORT.md:25); sufficient condition only, 'not a conclusion for all O(N)-relative-entropy preparations' (REPORT.md:90)

### s07_wm_b1_b2 — WM / B1 / B2 matter and moving channels (preparation acceptance, recoverability, delivery, retention; B2 feasibility stop)  (`s07_wm_b1_b2`)

**Question asked.** In a finite closed quantum model whose bath for the matter subsystem is the rest of the same model (recorded geometry word, storage bits, tail registers and the retained 64N^2-mode conditional receiver with n_R=Q-1, never reset), can one supplied time-independent Hamiltonian (i) start from an accepted prepared record, (ii) convert fuel to matter, (iii) deliver it to a readout region, (iv) retain it throughout a stated window, and (v) keep an encoded input recoverable — and does any of that force a law beyond ordinary quantum mechanics? Closeout sub-question: does any currently available quantitative route establish the moving-geometry thresholds (output >=1/10, retention >=1/4, conditional information error <=1/100) within the authorized compute envelope? (SPECIALIST_REVIEW.md:15-56,85-88; MOVING_GEOMETRY.md:7-41; STOP_DECISION.md:6-13; B1 REPORT.md:13-14)

**Assumptions / model class.**
- Supplied law and constants: hard-core plus/minus species, three-level electric links with +sum E^2 and Gauss law, neutral fuel slots, 64N^2-mode bosonic receiver with nearest-mode element -1, g in [0.9,1.1], delta_i=bar_delta+epsilon_i with |bar_delta|<=1/2, |epsilon_i|<=1/4; jp=1/64, jm=1/96, A=13/46080, J=lambda=1/3840, Omega=2-2A (SPECIALIST_REVIEW.md:17-56; consolidation THEOREM.md:9-33)
- Recording, fuel loading and the accepted record are supplied, 'not autonomous formation claims' (SPECIALIST_REVIEW.md:60-70; MOVING_GEOMETRY.md:41; P1 REPORT.md:3)
- Readout windows [L/(8J),L/(6J)] and [2/J,L/(6J)], decoder, and thresholds 1/10, 1/4, 1/100 are supplied (SPECIALIST_REVIEW.md:75-88)
- Every derivation is author-derived, independent review pending: 'All prior derivations are author-derived, independent review pending. No external reviewer has been contacted.' (SPECIALIST_REVIEW.md:4-5); the internal Challenger shared ancestry and 'is internal challenge, not independent mathematical review' (REVIEW_STATUS.md:3-7; consolidation CHALLENGE.md:1-6; B1 CHALLENGE_AND_INTEGRATION.md:3-5)
- Closeout computational envelope: at most 25 s per child, 60 combined child seconds, 1 GiB memory, 25 MiB new files (FEASIBILITY.md:84-86)
- Preparation acceptance inherits S9's unreviewed threshold N>=2^28, L=64N^2, 4N^2<=t<=8N^2 => Pr(m>=ceil(N/2))>1/2 (wm2_physical_regime REPORT.md:7-8; 'author-derived; independent mathematical review is still pending', wm2_proof_matter_challenge REPORT.md:11-12)
- Fixed-path family L in 24N, L>=2016 for B2-RA; whole-word moving family N>=2^28, L>=N/2 (FEASIBILITY.md:66-69; SPECIALIST_REVIEW.md:60-63)
- P1's matter-number persistence is a supplied control: conversion is switched off at operation start; 'keeping conversion on is not proved stable' (P1 REPORT.md:31-35). A1 tests leaving conversion on.

- A1: autonomous every-time retention gate refuted at one permitted point; matter returns to fuel with total energy conserved; neutral band module survives; methods are conventional quantum physics — `research/reality_framework/grut_a1_fixed_conversion_20261017/REPORT.md:3-8,14-16,29-30,36-53,72,78-91,96-107,118-120,126-129` — gate Pr(P(t)>=N/64)>=1/4 every t in [pi/2,pi/2+N/65536], N>=2^18; point Omega=13/4, lambda=1, Pr(P(pi/2)>=K)>0.946-2D_A; Pr(P(pi)>=K)<0.215+2D_A<1/4; D_A<=exp(-N/5)+exp(-N/12), one cell 0.999999995 at pi/2, 0.00065247 at pi; 773 states ~1 / 0.00345813; energy within 5.4e-15, 0.9872 s, 121.41 MiB, t_0=1/3840, dispersion error <8.1e-6; initial band weight <=1/529
- WM2 line one-line verdicts (13 packets): formation/recovery stops, conditional constructive results, negatives on universal locality and shared geometry — `research/reality_framework/wm2_*/REPORT.md:1-12 (each; head -40 skimmed)` — S1: closed seeded dynamics fail appreciable formation (wm2_defect_assembly:6-10), S2: resolves S1 at a supplied exact resonance; rejects robust untuned phase; branching proposal reopens a constant-length protected shortcut — 'two architecture failures' (wm2_fuel_lock:3-12), S3: finite-receiver stability interval; does not retain S2's consistency band (wm2_detuning_receiver:1-11), S4: prepared bulk protection; large-size disordered formation open (wm2_prepared_disorder:1-8), S5: five-event bounds above 77% and 80%; six-event unresolved (wm2_history_formation:5-9), S6: six-event formation at T=144 unresolved (wm2_conditioned_receiver:8), S7: five-event recovery passes; six-event computationally unresolved (wm2_positive_residual:3-8), S8: both recovery components stop before target (wm2_schmidt_receiver:3-7), S9: N>=2^28, L=64N^2, 4N^2<=t<=8N^2 => Pr(m>=ceil(N/2))>1/2; bounded finite recovery fails (wm2_physical_regime:3-8), proof/matter challenge: shared geometry not enforced; S9 self-check is 'the same Builder checking its work' (wm2_proof_matter_challenge:3-12), UC1: negative in frozen class U; universal locality fails (wm2_charge_constraint:1-8), IG1: observables do not select the interaction law; constraints do not force information locality (wm2_generator_classification:1-10), D1: P_N=N/16, N_A=N_B=N/32, |t|<=N/4096, error<=exp(-N/5)+3exp(-N/17) (wm2_many_carrier:10-12)

**Answer.** Within the supplied-Hamiltonian class this sector established: an externally powered, conditional preparation with conversion switched off at operation start (P1); a size-uniform refutation of autonomous every-time retention at one permitted parameter point (A1); a conditional one-composite delivery/retention window at author-derived status (B1); an author-derived fixed-path two-source pass (>12.852225% output, >43.4281% retention, exact conditional channel) for L in 24N, L>=2016 (B2-RA); exact reasons that pass does not transfer to the moving law (buffer hypothesis fails at t=0 with d_buffer=-1, witnesses J jp=1/245760 and J jm=1/368640, lost endpoint order identity, horizon 640L); an exact instrument reduction to Gram entries u,v,c,u_T,v_T (B2-IS); an optimal color-only recovery formula plus a strict small-diagnostic obstruction to perfect four-dimensional recovery (MC-E1); and a documented stop because every available certificate (20L, 1411L, dense 2^66 bytes) is vacuous or out of envelope at N>=2^28. The moving-geometry operating question is unresolved: neither established nor refuted. The original eight-event B2 endpoint remains failed (joint outgoing 0.01932660 to 0.01932751 against >=1/10; contact-cancel control 0.00550774 to 0.00550942 against >=1/100), while its conditional information channel was exact. The four notions stay distinct and are never multiplied: acceptance is conditional on unreviewed S9; recoverability is exact only conditionally (fixed path) or for a qutrit message; delivery and retention are proved only on the fixed path or the B1 one-composite window; P1's persistence is a supplied control. The closeout is a feasibility stop within a stated envelope (1411/640 majorant, N=2^28, L=134,217,744, T=85,899,356,160, 2^62 modes, 2^66 bytes vs 2^30), explicitly 'unresolved, not refuted' and a 'method/resource decision'. 'Incompatible state spaces not composable' is supported only in the specific forms the sources state (same-space comparison hypothesis fails; no invariant endpoint embedding; small diagnostics are not the physical space; one-resource channels do not tensorize); B2-IS says departure from the fixed-word sector can be operationally harmless when the Gram matrix is preserved, and the sources leave a future same-space proof open.

**Closure verdict.** Nothing additional beyond known physics was forced in this class, and the sector's own computations show it. Every result is finite closed-system quantum mechanics applied to a supplied Hamiltonian with supplied preparation and readout: 'conventional quantum physics ... not an earned new law of nature' (A1 REPORT.md:118-120); 'The same leading predictions follow from a conventional tight-binding channel. Empirical distinction remains absent.' (B1 CHALLENGE_AND_INTEGRATION.md:19); 'No distinct empirical prediction follows.' (diagnosis ARCHITECTURAL_VERDICT.md:39); 'There is no new physical result' (REVIEW_STATUS.md:23). What closure (bath = rest of the model, never reset) did do, as recorded in the sources — the grouping below is this ledger's synthesis, not a source statement: (1) with no external sink, retention cannot be bought from the environment — A1's matter returns to fuel, 'No energy or physical system disappears' (A1 REPORT.md:51-53), 'Total energy thereafter is conserved.' (A1 REPORT.md:72); B1's surviving route is internal spatial transport in a finite window subject to recurrence (B1 FAILURE_MODES.md:36-39) — known finite-system physics; (2) because the complement stays in the law, every operating estimate must control it, which is why the fixed-path/buffer proof fails to compose (d_buffer=-1, MOVING_GEOMETRY.md:87-88) and why dense computation is out of envelope (2^62 modes, FEASIBILITY.md:72-75); no packet supplies a new dynamical principle; (3) the information question reduces exactly to Gram entries of the closed evolution with a single SWAP primitive (B2-IS REPORT.md:36-44) and to an author-derived optimal-fidelity formula (MC-E1 REPORT.md:10-19; external review pending, :3). No GRUT-specific law, selection rule, prediction or gravity statement was earned here (P1 REPORT.md:134-136; B1 REPORT.md:59-61; B2-RA REPORT.md:69-72; MC-E1 REPORT.md:102-103; STOP_DECISION.md:63).

**Classification.** `mixed` — Recovered known physics: Rabi conversion, local perturbation bounds, bound-pair bands (A1 REPORT.md:118-120), tight-binding transport (B1 CHALLENGE_AND_INTEGRATION.md:19), finite recurrence (B1 FAILURE_MODES.md:36-39), two-vector Gram recovery fidelity (MC-E1 REPORT.md:10-19, author-derived). Conditional constraints: B2-RA bounds for L in 24N, L>=2016 (consolidation REPORT.md:16-26); P1 bound for N multiple of 64, N>=256 (P1 REPORT.md:46-53); B1 window for >=64 events (B1 REPORT.md:16-26); D1 regime (wm2_many_carrier REPORT.md:10-12); S5 five-event bounds (wm2_history_formation REPORT.md:5-7). Supplied model choices: conversion law, fuel energy, contact, degree counterterm, color symmetry, recording, loading, decoder, thresholds, P1's conversion-off control (B1 REPORT.md:55-57; SPECIALIST_REVIEW.md:54-56,70; P1 REPORT.md:31-42). Rejected mechanisms: autonomous A1 retention at Omega=13/4, lambda=1 (A1 REPORT.md:3-8,29-30); inherited empty-buffer composition for moving B2 (MOVING_GEOMETRY.md:85-95); gap-only two-level alternative (B1 FAILURE_MODES.md:3-15); dense enumeration within envelope (FEASIBILITY.md:72-75); positive-access and absolute-current routes as currently available (STOP_DECISION.md:17-23); S2's robust untuned phase and branching shortcut (wm2_fuel_lock REPORT.md:7-12); universal locality in class U (wm2_charge_constraint REPORT.md:3-8). Conjecture/unresolved: moving-geometry operation at N>=2^28 (STOP_DECISION.md:62); many-carrier theory (B1 REPORT.md:66); B2-AS source hypotheses (B2-AS REPORT.md:24-29). Unexecuted: independent review of every author-derived proof; Eq.(3) comparison (MOVING_GEOMETRY.md:178-183); bounds on u,v,c,u_T,v_T (SPECIALIST_REVIEW.md:92-101); specialist packet prepared but 'no sending action was taken' (REVIEW_STATUS.md:24-25); B2-AS assistance request with 'No reviewer has been contacted' (B2-AS REPORT.md:94-98); original B2 'prepared review request has not been sent' (collision_channel REPORT.md:88-89); six-event formation targets in S5/S6/S7/S8.

**Remaining freedom.**
- A phase-sensitive estimate of the signed kernel R U_-(t-s) T_X U_+(s) xi_w, or a certified compressed/analytic moving-window representation (FEASIBILITY.md:28-29,76-78; SPECIALIST_REVIEW.md:103-109)
- The same-space comparison Eq.(3) with epsilon_geo<=1/1000, which would yield p_out>0.12780625, p_ret>0.432964, error<=2/715 (MOVING_GEOMETRY.md:171-199); or unnormalized-operation errors eta_R=eta_T=1/1000 giving >0.12752225, >0.433281, <=4000/510089 (B2-IS REPORT.md:72-75)
- Other parameter points or observation protocols for autonomous retention; A1 refutes uniformity at one point only; a successor must defeat the local return estimate with a specified mechanism (A1 REPORT.md:46-47,126-129)
- MC-E1-T restricted qutrit message protocol with exact conditional identity; its u>=.1 and u_T>=.25 remain to be shown (MC-E1 REPORT.md:61-72)
- Reopening conditions for the B2 moving branch: an explicit quantitatively adequate route, a substantive proof correction, or independent mathematical assistance (STOP_DECISION.md:69-70)
- Not free: a following-head program, new recording time, favorable receiver, time averages or pinching, new thresholds (MOVING_GEOMETRY.md:32-35; SPECIALIST_REVIEW.md:64-65,117-118; A1 REPORT.md:16; diagnosis REPORT.md:38)

**Must not be promoted.**
- >12.852225% output and >43.4281% retention are fixed-path, L in 24N, L>=2016, author-derived, unreviewed bounds; not moving-geometry results (consolidation REPORT.md:16-31; THEOREM.md:147-151)
- MOVING_GEOMETRY margins 0.12780625, 0.432964, 2/715 are conditional on an unachieved epsilon_geo<=1/1000 (MOVING_GEOMETRY.md:185-193); B2-IS margins >0.12752225, >0.433281, <=4000/510089 on unachieved eta=1/1000 (B2-IS REPORT.md:72-75); B2-AS >11.458225%, >30.75% on unproved source hypotheses (B2-AS REPORT.md:24-27)
- 40,320; 2,684,354,880; 2,844,576; 189,381,236,784 are failed error certificates, not error estimates or failure probabilities (STOP_DECISION.md:45-48); 5.55e-24 is a sufficient population-majorant requirement for one method, 'not a necessary condition' (STOP_DECISION.md:39-43)
- 2^66 bytes is a dense-storage count, not a memory lower bound for every algorithm (FEASIBILITY.md:74-75)
- |gamma| about 0.99999583, 0.751352, 0.996551, 0.879595, 0.99774997, 0.32619522 are finite-control observations at specific times, not all-size theorems or replacement readouts (diagnosis REPORT.md:51-56; B1 REPORT.md:40-42; collision_channel REPORT.md:35-37)
- 70.46% is a per-fuel marginal, not a joint both-full probability (diagnosis REPORT.md:19)
- S9's N>=2^28 acceptance and P1's Pr(accepted AND P>=N/32)>1/4 are conditional on the unreviewed S9 theorem (P1 REPORT.md:59-67)
- MC-E1-T exact identity is for a qutrit message, not the original two-qubit task (MC-E1 REPORT.md:69-72)
- B1's >3/10 per original seed trial is conditional on S9 surviving review (B1 REPORT.md:30-32)
- No empirical prediction, gravity statement or theory-of-reality claim from any packet in this sector (P1 REPORT.md:134-136; B1 REPORT.md:59-61; ARCHITECTURAL_VERDICT.md:39; MC-E1 REPORT.md:102-103; REVIEW_STATUS.md:23; B2-RA REPORT.md:69-72)

**Handoff-row discrepancies found.**
- 'Results from incompatible state spaces are not composable' (NEXT_CHAT_HANDOFF.md:120) is a paraphrase occurring in no primary source. A repo-wide grep finds it only in the handoff row and in the derived ledger files research/reality_framework/grut_closed_whole_theory_20261019/CAMPAIGN_LEDGER.md:922,960 and ledger/ledger_s07_wm_b1_b2.md (written from the earlier reader's entry after 96a7e7e; not primary). Sources make narrower claims: same-space comparison hypothesis fails at t=0 with d_buffer=-1 (MOVING_GEOMETRY.md:85-95); no invariant endpoint embedding for m<N (B2-RB REPORT.md:29-30); small diagnostics are not the physical state space (MC-E1 REPORT.md:81-82; P1 REPORT.md:116-117; A1 REPORT.md:90-91); one-resource channels do not tensorize (B1 FAILURE_MODES.md:43-45; CHALLENGE_AND_INTEGRATION.md:17); probabilities are not multiplied (diagnosis REPORT.md:19-21; MOVING_GEOMETRY.md:199; ARCHIVE_STATUS.md:27-28). Against the row as a general principle: 'Large departure from the old fixed-word sector can therefore be operationally harmless' when the Gram matrix is preserved (B2-IS REPORT.md:29-32); 'A new proof may work' (consolidation REPORT.md:93-94); 'They are not a theorem that no future proof can do so.' (MOVING_GEOMETRY.md:202-203). The row over-generalizes.
- 'original WM/B reports via the continuation/index documents' resolves to no path: find for *continuation* returns nothing; the only *index* files are GRUT_RECORD_RECONCILIATION_INDEX_01.md (root) and the closeout's SOURCE_INDEX.json; GRUT_RECORD_RECONCILIATION_INDEX_01.md and NO_GO_LEDGER.md contain 0 matches for B2|WM2|B1|B2-RA. The WM/B reports were located by directory listing.
- The row frames the stop as 'within a specified computational envelope' only; the sources frame it as both a missing phase-sensitive method and a resource limit: 'Stop is a method/resource decision' (FEASIBILITY.md:89); 'More compute alone is not prescribed.' (STOP_DECISION.md:55). Incomplete rather than contradictory.
- The row omits that the fixed-path pass B2-RA is itself author-derived with independent review pending (STOP_DECISION.md:59; consolidation REPORT.md:5-6), that the original eight-event B2 endpoint remains failed (ARCHIVE_STATUS.md:33; collision_channel REPORT.md:20), and that A1's autonomous retention gate is refuted (A1 REPORT.md:3). A reader of the row alone could take the feasibility stop to be the only open item.
- The row's first sentence (keep preparation acceptance, recoverability, delivery and retention distinct) is consistent with the sources (MC-E1 REPORT.md:99-101; SPECIALIST_REVIEW.md:70-73; diagnosis REPORT.md:19-21). No discrepancy.
- The row's only source link is REVIEW_STATUS.md, which mentions the 1411/640 majorant and N=2^28 (REVIEW_STATUS.md:10-11) but none of the other envelope numbers; those are in FEASIBILITY.md, FEASIBILITY.json and STOP_DECISION.md. Omission, not contradiction.

### s08_candidate1 — Candidate 1 scalar EFT (conventional Einstein/SM-plus-scalar candidate)  (`s08_candidate1`)

**Question asked.** Once a conventional closed law of this class (Einstein gravity + Standard Model with Dirac neutrinos + an added X/phi/D scalar sector) is fully specified and its charged organized object is computed classically, at one scalar loop, and under rotating fluctuations, does anything beyond known physics get forced? The packets' own framing was whether such a candidate could be the intended universal theory; their recorded outcome is "a narrower working model rather than the intended universal theory" (research/reality_framework/grut_candidate_1_20261018/REPORT.md:7), and the closure-fork reassessment states the loop coefficient "does not supply a new physical principle" (research/reality_framework/grut_closure_fork_20261019/REPORT.md:5-8).

**Assumptions / model class.**
- Seven supplied postulates P1-P7: oriented, time-oriented, globally hyperbolic Lorentzian 4-manifold with spin structure; complex amplitudes, unitary perturbative EFT evolution, Born probabilities; SM + 3 nu_R + real phi, D and complex X; U(1)_X x Z2_phi x Z2_D and EFT B-L; Einstein action with reduced Planck mass; quartic potential with g_X^4 > lambda_phi lambda_X; explicit matching/preparation prescription (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:25-33). Dimension, orientation, gravity and Born rule are stated as supplied, not emergent (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:27-31; COMPATIBILITY.md:52; OPEN_CLAIMS.md:34).
- Action S_0 = int d^4x sqrt(-g)[M_P^2 R/2 - rho_Lambda + L_SM+nu_R - (1/2)(grad phi)^2 - |grad X|^2 - (1/2)(grad D)^2 - U] + S_boundary (GHY plus ADM reference subtraction), with U = (lambda_phi/4)(phi^2-v^2)^2 + g_X^2 phi^2 |X|^2 + lambda_X |X|^4 + (1/2)(m_D0^2 + h_D^2 phi^2) D^2 + (lambda_D/4) D^4; vacuum masses m_X=g_X v, m_phi=sqrt(2 lambda_phi) v, m_D=sqrt(m_D0^2+h_D^2 v^2) (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:41-62).
- EFT completion rule: fixed covariant gauge, dimensional regularization, MSbar at mu_0 = g_X v; all symmetry-allowed counterterms admitted; renormalized xi's, m_X0^2, extra portals, curvature-squared and higher Wilson coefficients set to zero at mu_0 as matching choices that run ('not symmetry protection or naturalness results'); illustrative cutoff Lambda_EFT = 0.1 M_P (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:86-94).
- Supplied benchmark couplings g_X=.1, lambda_phi=.01, lambda_X=.001, h_D=.01, m_D0=g_X v, lambda_D=.001, v/M_P=10^-5, 'a declared illustrative point, not fitted or measured parameters of nature' (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:211; REPORT.md:35).
- Asymptotically flat benchmark with renormalized rho_Lambda=0, 'not a cosmological prediction' (REPORT.md:35); 'not a prediction that the observed cosmological constant vanishes' (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:55).
- Mechanism is admitted prior art: 'a quartic extension of the Friedberg-Lee-Sirlin/non-topological-soliton construction'; 'not an originality claim for GR, Q-balls, quantum mechanics, or scalar detectors' (GRUT_CANDIDATE_1.md:217); Heeck-Sokhashvili cited, numerical bounds not borrowed (stability REPORT.md:40).
- All quantum calculations are flat-space new-scalar loops only; SM, graviton and finite-temperature/density loops excluded; SM sector sits in its vacuum (quantum REPORT.md:39; energy REPORT.md:51-52; COMPATIBILITY.md:22).
- The one-loop fixed-charge energy is conditional on stability of the reduced continuum saddle, which finite-box spectra and determinant samples 'support, but do not prove' (energy REPORT.md:24-26; binding_results.json:77).
